\subsection{Bayesian Inference}\label{bayesian-inference}

\[T\sim Binom(170, \pi)\]

\CommentTok{\#second deriv is positive??}

\[f(t)={170 \choose t}\pi^t(1-\pi)^{170-t} \ t=0,1,2,...,170\]
\[\pi=\frac{1-\psi}{2-\psi}\]
\[f(t)={170 \choose t}(\frac{1-\psi}{2-\psi})^{t}(\frac{1}{2-\psi})^{170-t}\]
\[L(\psi)={170 \choose 8}(\frac{1-\psi}{2-\psi})^8(\frac{1}{2-\psi})^{162}\]
\[\ell(\psi)=ln{170 \choose 8}+8ln(1-\psi)-8ln(2-\psi)+162ln1-162ln(2-\psi)\]
\[\frac{d}{d\psi}\ell(\psi)=0-\frac{8}{1-\psi}+\frac{8}{2-\psi}+0+\frac{162}{2-\psi}=0\]
\[\frac{170}{2-\psi}=\frac{8}{1-\psi}\] \[170-170\psi=16-8\psi\]
\[154=162\psi\] \[\widehat{\psi}_0^{mle}=\frac{154}{162}\approx 0.9506\]
\#\# Second Derivative Test

\[\frac{d^2}{d\psi^2}\ell(\psi)=-\frac{8}{(1-\psi)^2}+\frac{170}{(2-\psi)^2}\]
